    “Prior to the rāsa dance, Lord Kṛṣṇa hid Himself in a grove just to have fun. When the gopīs came, their eyes resembling those of deer, by His sharp intelligence He exhibited His beautiful four-armed form to hide Himself. But when Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī came there, Kṛṣṇa could not maintain His four arms in Her presence. This is the wonderful glory of Her love.”
    Purport

    This is a quotation from the Ujjvala-nīlamaṇi (Nāyikābheda 7), by Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī.

    — A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda

    Caitanya-caritāmṛta 17.300

    All the devotees of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, headed by Śrīvāsa Ṭhākura, have their own emotional humors in which they render service unto Him.
    📝 My note

    Source: vedabase.io

    Caitanya-caritāmṛta 17.301

    ↗
    paṇḍita-gosāñi ādi yāṅra yei rasa sei sei rase prabhu hana tāṅra vaśa
    [pandita-gosaani aadi yaanra yei rasa sei sei rase prabhu hana taanra vasha]
    Blended reading (Sanskrit + English inline)
    paṇḍita-gosāñi ādi yāṅra yei rasa sei sei rase prabhu hana tāṅra vaśa
    Personal associates like Gadādhara, Svarūpa Dāmodara, Rāmānanda Rāya, and the six Gosvāmīs (headed by Rūpa Gosvāmī) are all situated in their respective transcendental humors. Thus the Lord submits to various positions in various transcendental mellows.
    Purport

    In verses 296 through 301 the emotional devoted service of Śrī Nityānanda, Śrī Advaita Prabhu and others has been fully described. Describing such individual service, the Gaura-gaṇoddeśa-dīpikā (11-16) declares that although Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu appeared as a devotee, He is none other than the son of Nanda Mahārāja. Similarly, although Śrī Nityānanda Prabhu appeared as Lord Caitanya’s assistant, He is none other than Baladeva, the carrier of the plow. Advaita Ācārya is the incarnation of Sadāśiva from the spiritual world. All the devotees headed by Śrīvāsa Ṭhākura are His marginal energy, whereas the devotees headed by Gadādhara Paṇḍita are manifestations of His internal potency. Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, Advaita Prabhu and Nityānanda Prabhu all belong to the viṣṇu-tattva category. Because Lord Caitanya is an ocean of mercy, He is addressed as mahāprabhu, whereas Nityānanda and Advaita, being two great personalities who assist Lord Caitanya, are addressed as prabhu. Thus there are two prabhus and one mahāprabhu. Gadādhara Gosvāmī is a representative of a perfect brāhmaṇa spiritual master. Śrīvāsa Ṭhākura represents a perfect brāhmaṇa devotee. These five are known as the Pañca-tattva.

    — A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda
    📝 My note

    Source: vedabase.io

    Caitanya-caritāmṛta 17.302

    tihaṅ śyāma, — vaṁśī-mukha, gopa-vilāsī ihaṅ gaura — kabhu dvija, kabhu ta’ sannyāsī

    📝 My note

    Caitanya-caritāmṛta 17.304

    ↗
    sei kṛṣṇa, sei gopī, — parama virodha acintya caritra prabhura ati sudurbodha
    [sei krishna, sei gopee, — parama virodha achintya charitra prabhura ati sudurbodha]
    Blended reading (Sanskrit + English inline)
    sei kṛṣṇa, sei gopī, — parama virodha acintya caritra prabhura ati sudurbodha
    He is Kṛṣṇa, yet He has accepted the mood of the gopīs. How is it so? It is the inconceivable character of the Lord, which is very difficult to understand.
    Purport

    Kṛṣṇa’s accepting the part of the gopīs is certainly contradictory according to any mundane calculations, but the Lord, by His inconceivable character, may act like the gopīs and feel separation from Kṛṣṇa, although He is Kṛṣṇa Himself. Such a contradiction can be reconciled only in the Supreme Personality of Godhead because He has energy that is inconceivable (acintya), which can make possible that which is impossible to do (aghaṭa-ghaṭana-patīyasī). Such contradictions are very difficult to understand unless a devotee strictly follows the Vaiṣṇava philosophy under the direction of the Gosvāmīs. Therefore Kṛṣṇadāsa Kavirāja Gosvāmī ends every chapter with this verse: śrī-rūpa-raghunātha-pade yāra āśacaitanya-caritāmṛta kahe kṛṣṇadāsa “Praying at the lotus feet of Śrī Rūpa and Śrī Raghunātha, always desiring their mercy, I, Kṛṣṇadāsa, narrate Śrī Caitanya-caritāmṛta, following in their footsteps.” In a song by Narottama dāsa Ṭhākura it is stated: rūpa-raghunātha-pade ha-ibe ākutikabe hāma bujhaba se yugala-pīriti The conjugal love between Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa, which is called yugala-pīriti, is not understandable by mundane scholars, artists or poets. It is simply to be understood by devotees who strictly follow in the footsteps of the six Gosvāmīs. Sometimes so-called artists and poets try to understand the love affairs of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa, and they publish cheap books of poetry and pictures on the subject. Unfortunately, however, they do not understand the transcendental affairs of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa even to the smallest degree. They are simply meddling in a matter in which they are not fit even to enter.

    — A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda
    📝 My note

    Source: vedabase.io

    Caitanya-caritāmṛta 17.305

    ↗
    ithe tarka kari’ keha nā kara saṁśaya kṛṣṇera acintya-śakti ei mata haya

    Blended reading (Sanskrit + English inline)

    One cannot understand the contradictions in Lord Caitanya’s character by putting forward mundane logic and arguments. Consequently one should not maintain doubts in this connection. One should simply try to understand the inconceivable energy of Kṛṣṇa; otherwise one cannot understand how such contradictions are possible.

    Caitanya-caritāmṛta 17.307

    ↗
    tarke ihā nāhi māne yei durācāra kumbhīpāke pace, tāra nāhika nistāra
    [tarke ihaa naahi maane yei duraachaara kumbheepaake pache, taara naahika nistaara]
    Blended reading (Sanskrit + English inline)
    tarke ihā nāhi māne yei durācāra kumbhīpāke pace, tāra nāhika nistāra
    If one simply adheres to mundane arguments and therefore does not accept this, he will boil in the hell of Kumbhīpāka. For him there is no deliverance.
    Purport

    Kumbhīpāka, a type of hellish condition, is described in Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam (5.26.13), wherein it is said that a person who cooks living birds and beasts to satisfy his tongue is brought before Yamarāja after death and punished in the Kumbhīpāka hell. There he is put into boiling oil called kumbhī-pāka, from which there is no deliverance. Kumbhīpāka is meant for persons who are unnecessarily envious. Those who are envious of the activities of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu are punished in that hellish condition.

    — A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda
    📝 My note

    Source: vedabase.io

    Caitanya-caritāmṛta 17.308

    ↗
    acintyāḥ khalu ye bhāvā na tāṁs tarkeṇa yojayet prakṛtibhyaḥ paraṁ yac ca tad acintyasya lakṣaṇam
    [achintyaah khalu ye bhaavaa na taams tarkena yojayet prakritibhyah param yach cha tad achintyasya lakshanam]
    Blended reading (Sanskrit + English inline)
    acintyāḥ khalu ye bhāvā na tāṁs tarkeṇa yojayet prakṛtibhyaḥ paraṁ yac ca tad acintyasya lakṣaṇam
    “Anything transcendental to material nature is called inconceivable, whereas arguments are all mundane. Since mundane arguments cannot touch transcendental subject matters, one should not try to understand transcendental subjects through mundane arguments.”
    Purport

    This verse from the Mahābhārata (Bhīṣma-parva 5.22) is also quoted in the Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (2.5.93), by Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī.

    — A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda
    📝 My note

    Source: vedabase.io

    Caitanya-caritāmṛta 17.309

    ↗
    adbhuta caitanya-līlāya yāhāra viśvāsa sei jana yāya caitanyera pada pāśa
    [adbhuta chaitanya-leelaaya yaahaara vishvaasa sei jana yaaya chaitanyera pada paasha]
    Blended reading (Sanskrit + English inline)
    adbhuta caitanya-līlāya yāhāra viśvāsa sei jana yāya caitanyera pada pāśa
    Only a person who has firm faith in the wonderful pastimes of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu can approach His lotus feet.
    📝 My note

    Source: vedabase.io

    Caitanya-caritāmṛta 17.310

    ↗
    prasaṅge kahila ei siddhāntera sāra ihā yei śune, śuddha-bhakti haya tāra
    [prasange kahila ei siddhaantera saara ihaa yei shune, shuddha-bhakti haya taara]
    Blended reading (Sanskrit + English inline)
    prasaṅge kahila ei siddhāntera sāra ihā yei śune, śuddha-bhakti haya tāra
    In this discourse I have explained the essence of the devotional conclusion. Anyone who hears this develops unalloyed devotional service to the Lord.
    📝 My note

    Source: vedabase.io

    Caitanya-caritāmṛta 17.311

    ↗
    likhita granthera yadi kari anuvāda tabe se granthera artha pāiye āsvāda
    [likhita granthera yadi kari anuvaada tabe se granthera artha paaiye aasvaada]
    Blended reading (Sanskrit + English inline)
    likhita granthera yadi kari anuvāda tabe se granthera artha pāiye āsvāda
    If I repeat what is already written, I may thus relish the purport of this scripture.
    📝 My note

    Source: vedabase.io

    Caitanya-caritāmṛta 17.312

    ↗
    dekhi granthe bhāgavate vyāsera ācāra kathā kahi’ anuvāda kare vāra vāra
    [dekhi granthe bhaagavate vyaasera aachaara kathaa kahi’ anuvaada kare vaara vaara]
    Blended reading (Sanskrit + English inline)
    dekhi granthe bhāgavate vyāsera ācāra kathā kahi’ anuvāda kare vāra vāra
    We can see in the scripture Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam the conduct of its author, Śrī Vyāsadeva. After speaking the narration, he repeats it again and again.
    Purport

    At the end of Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam, in the Twelfth Canto, the twelfth chapter contains forty-three verses in which Śrī Kṛṣṇa-dvaipāyana Vedavyāsa recapitulates Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam’s entire subject matter. Śrī Kṛṣṇadāsa Kavirāja Gosvāmī wants to follow in the footsteps of Śrī Vyāsadeva by recapitulating the seventeen chapters of Śrī Caitanya-caritāmṛta’s Ādi-līlā.

    — A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda
    📝 My note

    Source: vedabase.io

    Caitanya-caritāmṛta 17.313

    ↗
    tāte ādi-līlāra kari pariccheda gaṇana prathama paricchede kailuṅ ‘maṅgalācaraṇa’
    [taate aadi-leelaara kari parichcheda ganana prathama parichchede kailun ‘mangalaacharana’]
    Blended reading (Sanskrit + English inline)
    tāte ādi-līlāra kari pariccheda gaṇana prathama paricchede kailuṅ ‘maṅgalācaraṇa’
    Therefore I shall enumerate the chapters of the Ādi-līlā. In the first chapter I offer obeisances to the spiritual master, for this is the beginning of auspicious writing.
    📝 My note

    Source: vedabase.io

    Caitanya-caritāmṛta 17.314

    ↗
    dvitīya paricchede ‘caitanya-tattva-nirūpaṇa’ svayaṁ bhagavān yei vrajendra-nandana
    [dviteeya parichchede ‘chaitanya-tattva-niroopana’ svayam bhagavaan yei vrajendra-nandana]
    Blended reading (Sanskrit + English inline)
    dvitīya paricchede ‘caitanya-tattva-nirūpaṇa’ svayaṁ bhagavān yei vrajendra-nandana
    The second chapter explains the truth of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. He is the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Kṛṣṇa, the son of Mahārāja Nanda.
    📝 My note

    Source: vedabase.io

    Caitanya-caritāmṛta 17.315

    ↗
    teṅho ta’ caitanya-kṛṣṇa — śacīra nandana tṛtīya paricchede janmera ‘sāmānya’ kāraṇa
    [tenho ta’ chaitanya-krishna — shacheera nandana triteeya parichchede janmera ‘saamaanya’ kaarana]
    Blended reading (Sanskrit + English inline)
    teṅho ta’ caitanya-kṛṣṇa — śacīra nandana tṛtīya paricchede janmera ‘sāmānya’ kāraṇa
    Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu, who is Kṛṣṇa Himself, has now appeared as the son of mother Śacī. The third chapter describes the general cause of His appearance.
    📝 My note

    Source: vedabase.io

    Caitanya-caritāmṛta 17.316

    ↗
    tahiṅ madhye prema-dāna — ‘viśeṣa’ kāraṇa yuga-dharma — kṛṣṇa-nāma-prema-pracāraṇa
    [tahin madhye prema-daana — ‘vishesha’ kaarana yuga-dharma — krishna-naama-prema-prachaarana]
    Blended reading (Sanskrit + English inline)
    tahiṅ madhye prema-dāna — ‘viśeṣa’ kāraṇa yuga-dharma — kṛṣṇa-nāma-prema-pracāraṇa
    The third chapter specifically describes the distribution of love of Godhead. It also describes the religion of the age, which is simply to distribute the holy name of Lord Kṛṣṇa and propagate the process of loving Him.
    📝 My note

    Source: vedabase.io

    Caitanya-caritāmṛta 17.317

    ↗
    caturthe kahiluṅ janmera ‘mūla’ prayojana sva-mādhurya-premānanda-rasa-āsvādana
    [chaturthe kahilun janmera ‘moola’ prayojana sva-maadhurya-premaananda-rasa-aasvaadana]
    Blended reading (Sanskrit + English inline)
    caturthe kahiluṅ janmera ‘mūla’ prayojana sva-mādhurya-premānanda-rasa-āsvādana
    The fourth chapter describes the main reason for His appearance, which is to taste the mellows of His own transcendental loving service and His own sweetness.
    📝 My note

    Source: vedabase.io

    Caitanya-caritāmṛta 17.318

    ↗
    pañcame ‘śrī-nityānanda’-tattva nirūpaṇa nityānanda hailā rāma rohiṇī-nandana
    [panchame ‘shree-nityaananda’-tattva niroopana nityaananda hailaa raama rohinee-nandana]
    Blended reading (Sanskrit + English inline)
    pañcame ‘śrī-nityānanda’-tattva nirūpaṇa nityānanda hailā rāma rohiṇī-nandana
    The fifth chapter describes the truth of Lord Nityānanda Prabhu, who is none other than Balarāma, the son of Rohiṇī.
    📝 My note

    Source: vedabase.io

    Caitanya-caritāmṛta 17.319

    ↗
    ṣaṣṭha paricchede ‘advaita-tattve’ra vicāra advaita-ācārya — mahā-viṣṇu-avatāra
    [shashtha parichchede ‘advaita-tattve’ra vichaara advaita-aachaarya — mahaa-vishnu-avataara]
    Blended reading (Sanskrit + English inline)
    ṣaṣṭha paricchede ‘advaita-tattve’ra vicāra advaita-ācārya — mahā-viṣṇu-avatāra
    The sixth chapter considers the truth of Advaita Ācārya. He is an incarnation of Mahā-Viṣṇu.
    📝 My note

    Source: vedabase.io

    Caitanya-caritāmṛta 17.320

    ↗
    saptama paricchede ‘pañca-tattve’ra ākhyāna pañca-tattva mili’ yaiche kailā prema-dāna
    [saptama parichchede ‘pancha-tattve’ra aakhyaana pancha-tattva mili’ yaiche kailaa prema-daana]
    Blended reading (Sanskrit + English inline)
    saptama paricchede ‘pañca-tattve’ra ākhyāna pañca-tattva mili’ yaiche kailā prema-dāna
    The seventh chapter describes the Pañca-tattva — Śrī Caitanya, Prabhu Nityānanda, Śrī Advaita, Gadādhara and Śrīvāsa. They all combined together to distribute love of Godhead everywhere.
    📝 My note

    Source: vedabase.io

    Caitanya-caritāmṛta 17.321

    ↗
    aṣṭame ‘caitanya-līlā-varṇana’-kāraṇa eka kṛṣṇa-nāmera mahā-mahimā-kathana
    [ashtame ‘chaitanya-leelaa-varnana’-kaarana eka krishna-naamera mahaa-mahimaa-kathana]
    Blended reading (Sanskrit + English inline)
    aṣṭame ‘caitanya-līlā-varṇana’-kāraṇa eka kṛṣṇa-nāmera mahā-mahimā-kathana
    The eighth chapter gives the reason for describing Lord Caitanya’s pastimes. It also describes the greatness of Lord Kṛṣṇa’s holy name.
    📝 My note

    Source: vedabase.io

    Caitanya-caritāmṛta 17.322

    ↗
    navamete ‘bhakti-kalpa-vṛkṣera varṇana’ śrī-caitanya-mālī kailā vṛkṣa āropaṇa
    [navamete ‘bhakti-kalpa-vrikshera varnana’ shree-chaitanya-maalee kailaa vriksha aaropana]
    Blended reading (Sanskrit + English inline)
    navamete ‘bhakti-kalpa-vṛkṣera varṇana’ śrī-caitanya-mālī kailā vṛkṣa āropaṇa
    The ninth chapter describes the desire tree of devotional service. Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu Himself is the gardener who planted it.
    📝 My note

    Source: vedabase.io

    Caitanya-caritāmṛta 17.323

    ↗
    daśamete mūla-skandhera ‘śākhādi-gaṇana’ sarva-śākhā-gaṇera yaiche phala-vitaraṇa
    [dashamete moola-skandhera ‘shaakhaadi-ganana’ sarva-shaakhaa-ganera yaiche phala-vitarana]
    Blended reading (Sanskrit + English inline)
    daśamete mūla-skandhera ‘śākhādi-gaṇana’ sarva-śākhā-gaṇera yaiche phala-vitaraṇa
    The tenth chapter describes the branches and subbranches of the main trunk and the distribution of their fruits.
    📝 My note

    Source: vedabase.io

    Caitanya-caritāmṛta 17.324

    ↗
    ekādaśe ‘nityānanda-śākhā-vivaraṇa’ dvādaśe ‘advaita-skandha śākhāra varṇana’
    [ekaadashe ‘nityaananda-shaakhaa-vivarana’ dvaadashe ‘advaita-skandha shaakhaara varnana’]
    Blended reading (Sanskrit + English inline)
    ekādaśe ‘nityānanda-śākhā-vivaraṇa’ dvādaśe ‘advaita-skandha śākhāra varṇana’
    The eleventh chapter describes the branch called Śrī Nityānanda Prabhu. The twelfth chapter describes the branch called Śrī Advaita Prabhu.
    📝 My note

    Source: vedabase.io

    Caitanya-caritāmṛta 17.325

    ↗
    trayodaśe mahāprabhura ‘janma-vivaraṇa’ kṛṣṇa-nāma-saha yaiche prabhura janama
    [trayodashe mahaaprabhura ‘janma-vivarana’ krishna-naama-saha yaiche prabhura janama]
    Blended reading (Sanskrit + English inline)
    trayodaśe mahāprabhura ‘janma-vivaraṇa’ kṛṣṇa-nāma-saha yaiche prabhura janama
    The thirteenth chapter describes the birth of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, which took place with the chanting of the holy name of Kṛṣṇa.
    📝 My note

    Source: vedabase.io

    Caitanya-caritāmṛta 17.326

    ↗
    caturdaśe ‘bālya-līlā’ra kichu vivaraṇa pañcadaśe ‘paugaṇḍa-līlā’ra saṅkṣepe kathana
    [chaturdashe ‘baalya-leelaa’ra kichu vivarana panchadashe ‘pauganda-leelaa’ra sankshepe kathana]
    Blended reading (Sanskrit + English inline)
    caturdaśe ‘bālya-līlā’ra kichu vivaraṇa pañcadaśe ‘paugaṇḍa-līlā’ra saṅkṣepe kathana
    The fourteenth chapter gives some description of the Lord’s childhood pastimes. The fifteenth briefly describes the Lord’s boyhood pastimes.
    📝 My note

    Source: vedabase.io

    Caitanya-caritāmṛta 17.327

    ↗
    ṣoḍaśa paricchede ‘kaiśora-līlā’ra uddeśa saptadaśe ‘yauvana-līlā’ kahiluṅ viśeṣa
    [shodasha parichchede ‘kaishora-leelaa’ra uddesha saptadashe ‘yauvana-leelaa’ kahilun vishesha]
    Blended reading (Sanskrit + English inline)
    ṣoḍaśa paricchede ‘kaiśora-līlā’ra uddeśa saptadaśe ‘yauvana-līlā’ kahiluṅ viśeṣa
    In the sixteenth chapter I have indicated the pastimes of the kaiśora age [the age prior to youth]. In the seventeenth chapter I have specifically described His youthful pastimes.
    📝 My note

    Source: vedabase.io

    Caitanya-caritāmṛta 17.328

    ↗
    ei saptadaśa prakāra ādi-līlāra prabandha dvādaśa prabandha tāte grantha-mukhabandha
    [ei saptadasha prakaara aadi-leelaara prabandha dvaadasha prabandha taate grantha-mukhabandha]
    Blended reading (Sanskrit + English inline)
    ei saptadaśa prakāra ādi-līlāra prabandha dvādaśa prabandha tāte grantha-mukhabandha
    Thus there are seventeen varieties of subjects in the first canto, which is known as the Ādi-līlā. Twelve of these constitute the preface of this scripture.
    📝 My note

    Source: vedabase.io

    Caitanya-caritāmṛta 17.329

    ↗
    pañca-prabandhe pañca-rasera carita saṅkṣepe kahiluṅ ati, — nā kailuṅ vistṛta
    [pancha-prabandhe pancha-rasera charita sankshepe kahilun ati, — naa kailun vistrita]
    Blended reading (Sanskrit + English inline)
    pañca-prabandhe pañca-rasera carita saṅkṣepe kahiluṅ ati, — nā kailuṅ vistṛta
    After the chapters of the preface, I have described five transcendental mellows in five chapters. I have described them very briefly rather than expansively.
    📝 My note

    Source: vedabase.io

    Caitanya-caritāmṛta 17.330

    ↗
    vṛndāvana-dāsa ihā ‘caitanya-maṅgale’ vistāri’ varṇilā nityānanda-ājñā-bale
    [vrindaavana-daasa ihaa ‘chaitanya-mangale’ vistaari’ varnilaa nityaananda-aajnaa-bale]
    Blended reading (Sanskrit + English inline)
    vṛndāvana-dāsa ihā ‘caitanya-maṅgale’ vistāri’ varṇilā nityānanda-ājñā-bale
    By the order and strength of Śrī Nityānanda Prabhu, Śrīla Vṛndāvana dāsa Ṭhākura has elaborately described in his Caitanya-maṅgala all that I have not.
    📝 My note

    Source: vedabase.io

    Caitanya-caritāmṛta 17.331

    ↗
    śrī-kṛṣṇa-caitanya-līlā — adbhuta, ananta brahmā-śiva-śeṣa yāṅra nāhi pāya anta
    [shree-krishna-chaitanya-leelaa — adbhuta, ananta brahmaa-shiva-shesha yaanra naahi paaya anta]
    Blended reading (Sanskrit + English inline)
    śrī-kṛṣṇa-caitanya-līlā — adbhuta, ananta brahmā-śiva-śeṣa yāṅra nāhi pāya anta
    The pastimes of Lord Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu are wonderful and unlimited. Even personalities like Lord Brahmā, Lord Śiva and Śeṣa Nāga cannot find their end.
    📝 My note

    Source: vedabase.io

    Caitanya-caritāmṛta 17.332

    ↗
    ye yei aṁśa kahe, śune sei dhanya acire milibe tāre śrī-kṛṣṇa-caitanya
    [ye yei amsha kahe, shune sei dhanya achire milibe taare shree-krishna-chaitanya]
    Blended reading (Sanskrit + English inline)
    ye yei aṁśa kahe, śune sei dhanya acire milibe tāre śrī-kṛṣṇa-caitanya
    Anyone who describes or hears any part of this elaborate subject will very soon receive the causeless mercy of Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu.
    📝 My note

    Source: vedabase.io

    Caitanya-caritāmṛta 17.333

    ↗
    śrī-kṛṣṇa-caitanya, advaita, nityānanda śrīvāsa-gadādharādi yata bhakta-vṛnda
    [shree-krishna-chaitanya, advaita, nityaananda shreevaasa-gadaadharaadi yata bhakta-vrinda]
    Blended reading (Sanskrit + English inline)
    śrī-kṛṣṇa-caitanya, advaita, nityānanda śrīvāsa-gadādharādi yata bhakta-vṛnda
    [Herein the author again describes the Pañca-tattva.] Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya, Prabhu Nityānanda, Śrī Advaita, Gadādhara, Śrīvāsa and all the devotees of Lord Caitanya.
    📝 My note

    Source: vedabase.io

    Caitanya-caritāmṛta 17.334

    ↗
    yata yata bhakta-gaṇa vaise vṛndāvane namra hañā śire dharoṅ sabāra caraṇe
    [yata yata bhakta-gana vaise vrindaavane namra hanaa shire dharon sabaara charane]
    Blended reading (Sanskrit + English inline)
    yata yata bhakta-gaṇa vaise vṛndāvane namra hañā śire dharoṅ sabāra caraṇe
    I offer my respectful obeisances to all the residents of Vṛndāvana. I wish to place their lotus feet on my head in great humbleness.
    📝 My note

    Source: vedabase.io

    Caitanya-caritāmṛta 17.335-336

    ↗
    śrī-svarūpa-śrī-rūpa-śrī-sanātana śrī-raghunātha-dāsa, āra śrī-jīva-caraṇaśire dhari vandoṅ, nitya karoṅ tāṅra āśa caitanya-caritāmṛta kahe kṛṣṇadāsa
    [shree-svaroopa-shree-roopa-shree-sanaatana shree-raghunaatha-daasa, aara shree-jeeva-charanashire dhari vandon, nitya karon taanra aasha chaitanya-charitaamrita kahe krishnadaasa]
    Blended reading (Sanskrit + English inline)
    śrī-svarūpa-śrī-rūpa-śrī-sanātana śrī-raghunātha-dāsa, āra śrī-jīva-caraṇaśire dhari vandoṅ, nitya karoṅ tāṅra āśa caitanya-caritāmṛta kahe kṛṣṇadāsa
    I wish to place the lotus feet of the Gosvāmīs on my head. Their names are Śrī Svarūpa Dāmodara, Śrī Rūpa Gosvāmī, Śrī Sanātana Gosvāmī, Śrī Raghunātha dāsa Gosvāmī and Śrī Jīva Gosvāmī. Placing their lotus feet on my head, always hoping to serve them, I, Kṛṣṇadāsa, narrate Śrī Caitanya-caritāmṛta, following in their footsteps.
    Purport

    Thus end the Bhaktivedanta purports to Śrī Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Ādi-līlā, seventeenth chapter, describing the pastimes of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu in His youth. END OF THE ĀDI-LĪLĀ

    — A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda
    📝 My note

    Source: vedabase.io
